````json
{
  "task_type": "diagnose:grpo_bf16_completion",
  "project": "faith-evidence-ie",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：诊断 wave1_grpo_bf16_g4 训练完成状态\n\nGRPO bf16 G=4 训练进程已退出，需要确认是正常完成还是异常退出。\n\n### Step 1: 检查日志\nSSH 到 rtx5090-4x，查看训练日志：\n```\ntail -100 /root/autodl-tmp/grpo_bf16_g4.log\n```\n\n关键信号：\n- 正常完成：应该看到 step 200/200 完成、保存 checkpoint、\"Training completed\" 等\n- 异常退出：OOM、CUDA error、Python traceback 等\n\n### Step 2: 检查 checkpoint\n```\nls -la /root/autodl-tmp/grpo_bf16_g4/\n```\n检查是否有 checkpoint-200（最终）以及之前的 checkpoint-100。\n\n### Step 3: 检查训练 metrics\n从日志中提取最终指标：\n- 最终 reward/mean\n- loss\n- total steps completed\n- 是否有任何 NaN/Inf 警告\n\n### Step 4: 检查 tmux session\n```\ntmux ls | grep grpo\n```\n确认 tmux session 是否还在。\n\n### Step 5: nvidia-smi 确认 cuda:2 状态\n```\nnvidia-smi\n```\n确认 cuda:2 现在是否空闲。\n\n### Step 6: 更新 registry\n根据诊断结果：\n- 如果正常完成 (step 200/200 + checkpoint saved)：\n  - `update_registry(exp_id=\"wave1_grpo_bf16_g4\", field=\"conclusion\", value=\"success\")`\n  - `update_registry(exp_id=\"wave1_grpo_bf16_g4\", field=\"metrics\", value={...})` 填入最终 metrics\n  - `update_registry(exp_id=\"wave1_grpo_bf16_g4\", field=\"spec\", value={\"checkpoint_path\": \"...\", \"log_path\": \"...\"})` 填入路径\n- 如果异常退出：\n  - `update_registry(exp_id=\"wave1_grpo_bf16_g4\", field=\"conclusion\", value=\"failed\")`\n  - `update_registry(exp_id=\"wave1_grpo_bf16_g4\", field=\"spec\", value={\"failure_reason\": \"...\"})` \n\n然后清除 cron 监控：\n- `delete_cron(\"wave1_grpo_bf16_g4\")`\n\n### 输出格式\n```\n=== 诊断结果 ===\n状态: [正常完成/异常退出]\n完成 steps: X/200\n最终 checkpoint: [路径]\ncuda:2: [空闲/占用]\n\n=== Metrics ===\nreward_mean: X\nloss: X\n(其他可用 metrics)\n\n=== Registry 操作 ===\nconclusion: [success/failed]\ncron: [已删除]\n```",
  "tool_groups": [
    "ssh",
    "registry"
  ],
  "tools": [
    "delete_cron"
  ]
}
````

<details open><summary>🔧 Tool call — Write · `toolu_01WBxz94vuazUD3JUteh14oa`</summary>

{
  "file_path": "/home/ubuntu/.claude-account-3/projects/-home-ubuntu--agent-ml-research-idea-gen-0513-6-projects-faith-evidence-ie--claude-sessions-agent-4b8948bb/memory/feedback_no_gpu_in_command.md",
  "content": "---\nname: No GPU=X in submit_training_job command\ndescription: Never pass GPU=X in submit_training_job command; tmux wrapper sets CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES, script fallback uses it\ntype: feedback\n---\nsubmit_training_job 命令中禁止传 `GPU=X`（如 `GPU=0 bash script.sh`）。\n\n**Why:** tmux wrapper 通过 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 映射物理 GPU（如 gpu_ids=[\"cuda:2\"] → CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=2）。脚本内 `export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=${GPU:-${CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES:-0}}` 在 GPU 未设置时正确使用 wrapper 的值。但如果命令中传了 `GPU=0`，会覆盖 wrapper 设置，导致实验跑在错误的 GPU 上。plan019_8b_sft_s44 因此跑到 cuda:0 而非 cuda:2。\n\n**How to apply:** 所有 submit_training_job 命令只写 `bash scripts/xxx.sh`，不加 `GPU=X` 前缀。GPU 分配完全由 submit_training_job 的 gpu_ids 参数 + tmux wrapper 控制。\n"
}
```

1	# Memory Index
2	
3	- [GPU Submit Discipline](feedback_gpu_submit.md) — Always nvidia-smi before submitting to any GPU; never trust Worker GPU claims
4	- [Batch Eval OOM](feedback_batch_eval_oom.md) — batch_eval --no-quantize + batch_size 16 OOMs on 32GB GPU; use batch_size 4
5	- [SFT Adapter in Eval](feedback_sft_adapter_eval.md) — All evals for models trained on SFT base MUST include --sft_adapter; missing it → rel_f1≈0
6	- [CED vs flat-NLI Config](feedback_ced_vs_flatnli_config.md) — CED/flat-NLI RSFT use different batch configs; never mix templates for paired t-test seeds
7	- [No-evidence Eval Flag](feedback_no_evidence_eval.md) — No-evidence evals MUST pass --no_evidence to inference.py; missing → prompt mismatch
8	- [GRPO data_dir parameter](feedback_grpo_data_dir.md) — GRPO uses --data_dir (not --data_path); docred_processor needs offline fix applied
9	- [SFT Eval Command Format](feedback_sft_eval_command.md) — SFT eval: --model_path=base_model --sft_adapter=adapter, NOT --model_path=adapter
10

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user operates as an ML research execution layer (Agent) for the "faith-evidence-ie" project (FREIGE: LLM-based evidence-grounded DocRE), targeting EMNLP 2026 Main (ARR July cycle, ~58 days). This session is a continuation from a previous context-exhausted session. The primary work involves:
   - Processing Director instructions for GPU task scheduling and experiment management
   - Harvesting eval results, updating registry, and immediately reporting to Director
   - Maintaining 4-GPU saturation on rtx5090-4x (GPU idle > 5 min = failure)
   - Managing Wave 1 experiments: SFT seeds, RSFT evaluations, GRPO training/eval
   - Fixing bugs (M007 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES, M008 GPU=X in commands, SFT eval command format)
   - Preparing scripts for scaling seeds (plan_019) and 14B model training (plan_017)
   - Following phased GPU scheduling plan: Phase 1 (evals) → Phase 2 (training) → Phase 3 (more evals)

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **FREIGE**: Evidence-grounded Document-level Relation Extraction using LLMs
   - **D076 Protocol**: Standardized eval (base+adapter, batch_size=4, no-quantize, seed=42, max_new_tokens=1024)
   - **D106 Conditional Termination**: GRPO ckpt-100 eval decides whether to continue to step 200. Threshold: ign_f1 vs SFT baseline (0.4057)
   - **Registry State Machine**: PREPARING → PENDING → QUEUED → ALLOCATED → RUNNING → ANALYZING → DONE. submit_training_job only creates PREPARING; must manually advance
   - **SFT Eval Command Format**: `--model_path=base_model --sft_adapter=adapter_path` (NOT --model_path=adapter)
   - **RSFT/GRPO Eval Format**: `--model_path=checkpoint --base_model=base --sft_adapter=sft_adapter`
   - **M007 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES fix**: Scripts changed from `${GPU:-0}` to `${GPU:-${CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES:-0}}`
   - **M008 GPU=X bug**: Never pass GPU=X in submit_training_job command; tmux wrapper handles CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES
   - **update_registry spec overwrite**: `update_registry(field="spec", value={...})` REPLACES entire spec; must pass complete object
   - **14B Training**: 2-GPU DeepSpeed ZeRO-3 bf16 LoRA (tensor parallelism not supported by project code), max_length=2048 safe (0.5% truncation)
   - **Scaling Seeds**: Need 3+ seeds per model scale for mean±std. Current 1.7B variance extreme (s42=0.4786, s44=0.4321, gap=4.65pp)

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **registry.yaml** (project truth source)
     - Continuously updated throughout session for experiment status, metrics, specs
     - Key experiments: wave1_grpo_bf16_g4 (DONE/success), plan019_1_7b_sft_s44 (DONE/success), random_rsft_k1_baseline (DONE/success), plan019_1_7b_sft_s43 (DONE/failed OOM), plan019_8b_sft_s44 (RUNNING cuda:0), plan019_1_7b_sft_s43_r2 (RUNNING cuda:2)
   
   - **feedback_no_gpu_in_command.md** (new memory file)
     - Created to record M008 lesson: GPU=X in submit_training_job overrides tmux wrapper
     - Content documents the bug where plan019_8b_sft_s44 ran on cuda:0 instead of cuda:2
   
   - **MEMORY.md** (memory index)
     - Updated to add M008 entry: `[No GPU=X in Command](feedback_no_gpu_in_command.md)`
   
   - **Scripts on rtx5090-4x** (modified by workers):
     - `scripts/seed_expansion/` — 10 scripts fixed for M007 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES
     - `scripts/seed_expansion/sft_14b.sh` — New 14B SFT script (2-GPU ZeRO-3, lr=1e-4)
     - `scripts/seed_expansion/eval_14b.sh` — New 14B eval script (2-GPU)
     - `scripts/seed_expansion/sft_8b_s44.sh` — New 8B SFT seed44 script
     - `scripts/seed_expansion/sft_llama_s44.sh` — New LLaMA SFT seed44 script
     - `scripts/seed_expansion/eval_8b_s44.sh` — New 8B RSFT eval seed44
     - `scripts/seed_expansion/eval_llama_s44.sh` — New LLaMA RSFT eval seed44
   
   - **resource-saturation.skill.md** (`/home/ubuntu/agent-ml-research/skills/global/`)
     - Read for GPU saturation reporting format: `[资源] GPU N/M | Worker K`

4. Errors and Fixes:
   - **update_registry spec overwrite (recurring)**:
     - Using `update_registry(field="spec", value={"gpu": ["cuda:2"]})` replaced entire spec with just gpu field
     - Lost command, server, pid, data_path, etc. for wave1_grpo_bf16_g4 and plan019_8b_sft_s44
     - Fix: Must pass complete spec object including all original fields when updating any spec field
   
   - **M008 GPU=0 in submit command**:
     - Submitted `GPU=0 bash scripts/seed_expansion/sft_8b_s44.sh` on cuda:2
     - Script's `export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=${GPU:-${CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES:-0}}` evaluated GPU=0, overriding tmux's CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=2
     - Result: 8B SFT s44 ran on cuda:0 instead of cuda:2
     - Fix: Never pass GPU=X in submit_training_job command. Saved to memory feedback.
     - Director confirmed: "M008 已记录。后续提交训练禁止额外传 GPU=X"
   
   - **Registry state machine not auto-advancing**:
     - submit_training_job creates experiments in PREPARING but doesn't advance
     - Must manually call update_registry for each transition: PREPARING→PENDING→QUEUED→ALLOCATED→RUNNING
     - Each transition requires a separate API call (4 calls per experiment)
   
   - **report.md not manually writable**:
     - Attempted save_project_file for report.md, got error: "file 'report.md' is not writable via save_project_file"
     - report.md is auto-generated
   
   - **main_agent_post_to_director timeout**:
     - One post timed out, retried with shorter message and succeeded
   
   - **plan019_1_7b_sft_s44 wrong checkpoint path in registry**:
     - Initially assumed `/root/autodl-tmp/sft_output_qwen3_1_7b_s44/`
     - Actual path: `/root/autodl-tmp/sft_output_qwen3_1_7b_bf16_seed44/`
     - Fixed by diagnostic worker discovery and registry update

5. Problem Solving:
   - **GPU saturation management**: Maintained 4/4 GPU utilization throughout by immediately filling freed GPUs with next priority tasks
   - **14B feasibility analysis**: Discovered tensor parallelism not supported → pivoted to ZeRO-3. Analyzed truncation risk → 0.5% safe at max_length=2048
   - **1.7B seed variance**: s44 eval showed ign_f1=0.4321 vs s42=0.4786 (4.65pp gap). 48% truncation rate identified as likely cause. Urgent third seed (s43) submitted for resolution.
   - **Phased GPU scheduling**: Adapted Director's Phase 1/2/3 plan dynamically as GPUs freed at different times than expected

6. All User Messages:
   - **[Session continuation summary]**: Detailed context from previous session including pending tasks, experiment states, GPU status
   - **[Director 指令] Reviewer 已独立核实 GPU 状态**: 3 GPUs idle, submit 3 evals (cuda:0 s44 eval, cuda:1 random RSFT eval, cuda:3 GRPO ckpt-100 eval), fix M007 bug, fix 4B eval, restart 1.7B s43, refresh report.md
   - **[WORKER DONE] worker_diagnose_gpu_status_and_collect_metrics**: Diagnostic results (s44 at different path, random_rsft completed, random_rsft.sh already partially fixed)
   - **[WORKER DONE] worker_fix_cuda_visible_devices_bug**: 10 scripts fixed in seed_expansion/
   - **[WORKER DONE] worker_eval_submit_3_evals_gpu_saturation**: 3 evals submitted (PIDs 558241, 558320, 558467)
   - **[Director 指令] 补充 registry 路径修正**: plan019_1_7b_sft_s44 checkpoint_path correction needed
   - **[Director 指令] 4 卡满载等 eval 结果，CPU 任务并行**: Fix cuda:2 GRPO registry, 14B model prep (plan_017), scaling seeds scripts (plan_019)

        "content": "---\nname: No GPU=X in submit_training_job command\ndescription: Never pass GPU=X in submit_training_job command; tmux wrapper sets CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES, script fallback uses it\ntype: feedback\noriginSessionId: 337a4a50-031e-44ff-82dc-16a2ce97f067\n---\nsubmit_training_job 命令中禁止传 `GPU=X`（如 `GPU=0 bash script.sh`）。\n\n**Why:** tmux wrapper 通过 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 映射物理 GPU（如 gpu_ids=[\"cuda:2\"] → CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=2）。脚本内 `export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=${GPU:-${CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES:-0}}` 在 GPU 未设置时正确使用 wrapper 的值。但如果命令中传了 `GPU=0`，会覆盖 wrapper 设置，导致实验跑在错误的 GPU 上。plan019_8b_sft_s44 因此跑到 cuda:0 而非 cuda:2。\n\n**How to apply:** 所有 submit_training_job 命令只写 `bash scripts/xxx.sh`，不加 `GPU=X` 前缀。GPU 分配完全由 submit_training_job 的 gpu_ids 参数 + tmux wrapper 控制。\n",
        "numLines": 12,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 12
      }
    },
    "displayPath": "../../../../../../.claude-account-3/projects/-home-ubuntu--agent-ml-research-idea-gen-0513-6-projects-faith-evidence-ie--claude-sessions-agent-4b8948bb/memory/feedback_no_gpu_in_command.md"

        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/agent-ml-research/skills/global/resource-saturation.skill.md",
        "content": "---\nname: resource-saturation\ndescription: \"当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。\"\nalways: false\nroles:\n  - director\n  - agent\n---\n\n# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）\n\n## A. GPU 分配率检查\n\n调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:\n- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号\n- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）\n- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️\n\n任何\"分配了但没跑\"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:\n- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备\n- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）\n- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报\"暂无可铺实验\"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作\n\n**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。\n\n## B. GPU 效率检查\n\n调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:\n- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务\n- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）\n- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优\n\n## C. 非 GPU 任务并行\n\nGPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:\n- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint\n- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置\n- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索\n- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合\n- 论文章节起草\n- 代码 review / refactor\n\n**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。\n\n## D. 汇报格式\n\n向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:\n\n    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)\n",
        "numLines": 50,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 50
      }

oracle ceiling 的 37% (0.0807/0.218)。CED-F1 Spearman r=0.3235 (p≈0)，相关性显著但中等——说明 CED 不是完美的 proxy 但有信号。Random baseline rel_f1=0.4538 说明 sampling diversity 本身就有 +4.8pp 收益，CED 在此基础上再加 +3.3pp。EDCR 下降
